\section[The Cosmological Constant]{The Cosmological Constant: Why the Vacuum Energy Is Not Zero---and Not Infinite}
\label{sec:cc}

The cosmological constant problem is sometimes described as ``the worst fine-tuning
problem in physics.''  Standard quantum field theory predicts a vacuum energy of order
$M_{\rm Pl}^4$; the observed value is smaller by a factor of $10^{122}$.
Any proposed cancellation mechanism requires adjusting two enormous numbers to agree
to 122 decimal places---a precision that seems to call for an explanation at a
deeper level.

In GTE the problem splits cleanly into three distinct levels.
Each level has a different mechanism, a different certification status, and
a different historical parallel.
The key insight is that the three levels are \emph{logically independent}:
the classical zero, the quantum suppression, and the value of the
nonzero residual are determined by three unrelated structural properties of the substrate.

\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=green!5,colframe=green!50!black,
  title={Three-Level Architecture: The CC Problem in GTE}]
\textbf{Level~1 --- Classical $\Lambda = 0$ (CatAL):}
The $\mathbb{Z}_7$ symmetry of the $\PHIMDL$ potential forces all seven vacuum
sectors to have equal action.  The vacuum energy is a topological invariant.
No classical contribution exists to cancel.

\smallskip
\textbf{Level~2 --- Quantum protection (CatAD):}
Three-tape holographic mode count $3L$ (not $L^3$) suppresses one-loop corrections
by $\sim 10^{-83}$ relative to the standard QFT prediction.

\smallskip
\textbf{Level~3 --- PSC adjudication floor (CatAD):}
PSC Reflexive Closure is undecidable.
The residual $\Dres > 0$ from the non-computable self-referential fragment
\emph{forces} $\OmL > 0$.
The specific value is bracketed by two structural routes.
\end{tcolorbox}

\subsection*{Level~1: Classical \texorpdfstring{$\Lambda = 0$}{Lambda = 0} (CatAL)}

The $\PHIMDL$ potential derived in Ch.~\ref{chap:field} has a $\mathbb{Z}_7$
winding symmetry: the field can sit in any of seven topologically inequivalent vacua,
related by the discrete shift $\phi \to \phi + 2\pi/7$.
As established in Ch.~\ref{chap:spacetime} (§\ref{sec:gr_lovelock}),
the $\ZS$ global shift symmetry is anomaly-free at the quantum level,
certified by three independent zero-sorry Lean theorems:
\lean{z7\_shift\_measure\_preserving}, \lean{z7\_jacobian\_eq\_one},
\lean{z7\_vacuum\_sectors\_equiprobable} (all \CatAL).

At every kink minimum the classical potential satisfies $V(\phi_k) = 0$
(\lean{phimdl\_tmunu\_vacuum\_zero}, \CatAL).
The vacuum energy is a topological invariant: $\partial V / \partial m^2 = 0$
(\lean{z7\_vacuum\_energy\_mass\_independent}, \CatAL).
All seven vacua remain quantum-degenerate to all loop orders by the anomaly-free
$\ZS$ symmetry.

The consequence for the cosmological constant is immediate:
\emph{there is no classical $\Lambda$ to cancel.}
The fine-tuning problem in its classical form does not arise because the $\ZS$
structure suppresses the classical vacuum energy to zero by symmetry, not by
any adjustment of parameters.

\subsection*{Level~2: Quantum Protection (\CatAD)}

One-loop QFT corrections typically contribute $\delta\rho_\Lambda \sim M_{\rm Pl}^4$
to the vacuum energy.
In GTE, the three-tape CMCA encodes 3D spacetime in 1D tapes (Ch.~\ref{chap:field}),
giving a holographic mode count of $3L$ cells---not the naive volume $L^3$.
This alone reduces the QFT mode density from $M_{\rm Pl}^3$ to $M_{\rm Pl}^2 H_0$,
bringing the vacuum energy estimate to $M_{\rm Pl}^2 H_0^2$ (the GTE natural unit for
the cosmological constant).

The holographic non-renormalization theorem (NRT) refines this further.
The ratio of loop to tree contribution is
\begin{equation}
  \frac{\delta\rho_{\rm loop}}{\rho_{\rm tree}}
  \approx \frac{3H_0^2}{M_{\rm kink}^2}
  \approx 7.4 \times 10^{-83},
\label{eq:nrt_ratio}
\end{equation}
machine-certified as an $H_0$-independent result
(\lean{holographic\_nrt\_loop\_tree\_ratio\_h0\_independent}, \CatAD).
For comparison, the standard QFT estimate gives $\delta\rho/\rho \approx 1.66 \times 10^{40}$.
The GTE holographic suppression is $83 + 40 = 123$ orders of magnitude below that.

The full path-integral proof---showing that the CMCA mode sum is exactly $3L$ and not
a divergent $L^3$---is gated on the Gromov--Hausdorff geometric continuum limit
(open quantum-gravity question OQ-QG-1) and remains \CatAD.

\subsection*{Level~3: The PSC Adjudication Floor (\CatAD)}

Classical $\Lambda = 0$ and quantum corrections are suppressed by 83 orders of magnitude.
Yet the observed $\OmL \approx 0.69$ is plainly nonzero.
This is the \emph{value problem}: why is $\OmL$ small but nonzero?

The GTE answer invokes the deepest structural property of the substrate:
\emph{PSC Reflexive Closure (RC) is undecidable.}

The connection to quantum mechanics is explicit.  In Ch.~\ref{chap:qm} we showed that
the same diagonal undecidability drives the Physical Incompleteness Theorem (PIT):
the universe cannot algorithmically predict all of its own measurement outcomes.
Here that same structural limit does something more: it forces the cosmological
constant to be nonzero.  The incompleteness that makes quantum measurement
non-computable is the same incompleteness that prevents $\OmL$ from being zero.
The two phenomena --- the irreducible randomness of individual measurement outcomes
and the nonzero energy of the vacuum --- share a single root cause.

PSC Layer~I Axiom~RC requires the substrate to encode its own complete description.
By the diagonal construction---the same argument as G\"{o}del's incompleteness theorem
applied to the physical substrate---this self-referential encoding cannot be completed
by any Turing-computable process.
Lean-certified: \lean{asr\_rt\_not\_computable} (\CatAL, from NEMS Paper~11).
The irreducible undecidable content leaves a residual $\Dres > 0$ on the effective
energy density.
This residual \emph{is} the cosmological constant.

The full logical chain is packaged as a single named theorem:

\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=orange!5,colframe=orange!60,
  title={Theorem: The PSC-Adjudication Theorem (\CatAL)}]
  PSC axioms $\;\Rightarrow\;$ physical incompleteness
  $\;\Rightarrow\;$ $\Dres > 0$
  $\;\Rightarrow\;$ $\OmL \neq 0$.\\[4pt]
  Zero sorry, zero axioms.
  The cosmological constant is the necessary consequence of the substrate being
  a PSC-consistent self-referential system.
\end{tcolorbox}

The connection between the \PSC\ adjudication floor and the cosmological constant
was unexpected.  I had not anticipated that the same formal incompleteness that governs
quantum measurement---the diagonal undecidability established in Ch.~\ref{chap:qm}---would
also set the scale of dark energy.  The derivation forces this conclusion without any
parameter adjustment: $\Dres > 0$ is the direct consequence of requiring the substrate
to satisfy \PSC\ Reflexive Closure.

Quantitatively, $\Dres = (\ln 2/\pi) \cdot \Lmodel \cdot H_0^2$, where
$\Lmodel = \log_2(2000/3)$ is the binary description length of the gauge-sector $\ZS$
orbit structure.
The orbit count is $2000/3 = D^2 N_{\rm fam}^{N_{\rm gen}} / N_{\rm gen} = 4^2 \cdot 5^3/3$
(CatAD, \lean{gauge\_spectrum\_total} \CatAL).
Then $\OmL = \Dres/(3H_0^2)$ follows from the Friedmann equation.

\subsection*{The 2.4\% Spread Is Transcendentally Irreducible}

The spread $|0.6899 - 0.6732| = 0.0167$ (2.4\%) between the two routes is not a failure
of the theory.
Route~1 involves the transcendental combination $\ln 2 \cdot \log_2(2000/3)$;
Route~2 involves $\pi$.
By the Baker--W\"{u}stholz theorem, $\ln 2 \cdot \log_2(2000/3)$ and $\pi$ are
algebraically independent over $\mathbb{Q}$: no bounded-degree polynomial relation
over the rationals can connect them.
The gap is mathematically irreducible---no refinement within GTE arithmetic can close it.

Physically, Route~1 uses \emph{discrete} information-theoretic counting while Route~2
uses \emph{continuous} holographic geometry.
They count genuinely different things.
That they agree to 2.4\% on a single observed physical quantity is a non-trivial
quantitative consistency between two independent formalisms.

\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=yellow!8,colframe=yellow!60!black,
  title={Why This Is Not Numerology}]

\item \textbf{Two independent mechanisms, no common calibration.}
  Route~1 and Route~2 share the structural atoms
  $(D, N_{\rm fam}, N_{\rm gen})$ but no common numerical constants.
\item \textbf{All four atoms are fixed independently of $\OmL$.}
  $D=4$ from DPP; $N_{\rm fam}=5$ from $Z_5$ orbit; $N_{\rm gen}=3$ from PSC Layer~II;
  $|\ZS|=7$ from MDL selection.
\item \textbf{Machine certification rules out post-hoc adjustment.}
  \lean{incompleteness\_implies\_nonzero\_omega\_lambda},
  \lean{d\_res\_determines\_omega\_lambda},
  and \lean{psp\_epoch\_selection\_master} are all zero-sorry;
  the derivation chain is fixed in Lean before comparison to observation.
\item \textbf{The bracket uniquely selects $N_{\rm gen} = 3$.}
  For all $N \in \{1,\ldots,11\}$ with $N \neq 3$,
  at least one route deviates from Planck 2018 by $\geq 5\sigma$.
  The bracket property is a unique signature of three generations.
\end{enumerate}
\end{tcolorbox}

\subsection*{The \texorpdfstring{$N_{\rm gen} = 3$}{N=3} Uniqueness Cross-Check (CatAD)}

The bracket property---both routes simultaneously containing the Planck 2018
central value---holds uniquely for $N_{\rm gen} = 3$.
The continuous minimum of $|\Omega_{\rm PSC}(N) - \Omega_{\rm holo}(N)|$ over
real $N$ lies at $N^* = 3.034$; the nearest integer is $N_{\rm gen} = 3$.
The same generation count forced by PSC Layer~II / Presentation Invariance
(\lean{psc\_enumeration\_forces\_ngen\_3}, \CatAL) is the unique integer satisfying
cosmological consistency.

This is a non-trivial internal cross-check between two unrelated physical principles:
generation selection (Ch.~\ref{chap:principle}) and dark-energy structure converge
on the same number without any common input.

\subsection*{Equation of State \texorpdfstring{$w = -1$}{w = -1} (CatAD)}

PSC epoch selection is a boundary condition, not a dynamical field.
The adjudication floor $\Dres$ is determined once, at the PSC boundary, by the
undecidable self-referential fragment.
It does not evolve: $d\rho_\Lambda/dt = 0$ by construction.
Energy conservation then forces $w = -1$ identically---no slow-roll field,
no quintessence, no time variation.

This predicts:
\begin{itemize}
\item $w_0 = -1$, $w_a = 0$ (equation-of-state parameters; \CatAD).
\item No dynamical dark energy at any redshift.
\item DESI Year~1 combined tension with $w=-1$: $1.37\sigma$---consistent with GTE.
\end{itemize}

Falsification criterion: $|w_0 + 1| > 0.040$ at $5\sigma$ (Euclid projected
threshold) would falsify the PSC boundary-condition interpretation of dark energy.

\section{The CMB Spectral Index}
\label{sec:ns}

The CMB scalar spectral tilt $\ns$ characterizes the scale-dependence of the primordial
density fluctuations that seeded all large-scale structure.
Its measured value $\ns = 0.9649 \pm 0.0042$ (Planck 2018) is one of the most precisely
measured cosmological observables.
The GTE prediction is:

\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=orange!5,colframe=orange!60,
  title={Result: CMB Spectral Index (\CatAL)}]
  \[
    \ns = 1 - \frac{\ln 2}{2\pi^2} = 0.96488,
  \]
  in agreement with Planck 2018 ($0.9649 \pm 0.0042$) at $+0.004\sigma$.
  Zero free parameters.
  Fourteen zero-sorry Lean theorems; module \lean{CMBSpectralTilt.lean}
  \cite{SpivackGTECosmology}.
\end{tcolorbox}

This is the single most precisely confirmed prediction of the framework.
The result $\ns = 0.96488$ at $+0.004\sigma$ from Planck 2018 remains, to my mind,
the most striking quantitative success: a number derivable from the binary entropy of
a single NAND gate and an algebraic cancellation in the Weyl law, agreeing with a
high-precision cosmological measurement to four decimal places.
The derivation is a four-step chain in which every algebraic step is certified at \CatAL.

\subsection*{The Physical Picture}

A \emph{scale-invariant} spectrum ($\ns = 1$) has equal power at every wavelength.
The observed red tilt $\ns = 0.9649 < 1$ means slightly less power at short wavelengths
than at long wavelengths.
In GTE, this tilt arises from the same running that governs the Newton coupling $G_N$.
The running rate $\betaG = \ln 2/(2\pi^2) = 0.03512$ pulls the spectrum away from scale
invariance.
The remarkable fact is that $\ln 2$ and $2\pi^2$ both enter for independent structural
reasons, and their ratio matches the observed tilt to four-decimal precision with no
tuning of any parameter.

\subsection*{Step~1: Binary Entropy $H(\mathbb{Z}_2) = \ln 2$}

The CMCA outer layer implements Rule~110 (right-chiral) and Rule~124 (left-chiral).
When the center cell $C = 1$, the update rule reduces to
$p(L,1,R) = 1 - L\cdot R = \mathrm{NAND}(L,R)$
(\lean{rule110\_center1\_is\_nand}, \CatAL).
NAND is functionally complete (Sheffer 1913): it is the universal binary operation
from which all other Boolean functions can be constructed.
The entropy of a fair NAND gate is $\ln 2$~nats---the entropy of a single unbiased
binary decision.
This is forced: there is no free parameter to choose $\ln 3$ or $\ln 7$.

\subsection*{Step~2: $\mathbb{Z}_2$/$\mathbb{Z}_7$ Separation}

The full $\PHIMDL$ field has a $\ZS$ winding structure with seven distinct
superselection sectors.
The Doplicher--Haag--Roberts (DHR) theorem, applied to the $\ZS$ winding
superselection sectors, forbids coherent perturbations \emph{across} $\ZS$ winding
sectors at the flat-metric level
(\lean{z7\_superselection\_preserved\_by\_flat\_metric}, \CatAL).
At cosmological scales where the metric is approximately flat, the $\ZS$ sectors
decohere and cannot interfere.
Only the $\ZT$ binary parity sublayer drives the primordial perturbation spectrum.

To see why this matters: if the full $\ZS$ field contributed,
\[
  \ns(\ZS)
  = 1 - \frac{\ln 7}{2\pi^2}
  \approx 1 - 0.099
  = 0.901,
\]
which deviates from Planck 2018 by $-15.11\sigma$---catastrophically wrong.
The $\ZS$ superselection argument is not a tuning; it is what makes the prediction work,
and it is certified at \CatAL.

\subsection*{Step~3: The Weyl Algebraic Miracle}

The number of modes with wavenumber $\leq k$ on the unit 3-sphere $S^3$ obeys
the Weyl law: $N(k) = k^3/3$ (\lean{weyl\_mode\_count\_eq}, \CatAL).
Taking the logarithmic derivative at the Planck scale $k_{\rm Pl} = 1$:
\[
  \left.\frac{dN}{d\ln k}\right|_{k_{\rm Pl}}
  = \frac{\mathrm{Vol}(S^3)}{2\pi^2}
  = \frac{2\pi^2}{2\pi^2}
  = 1.
\]
This exact cancellation---$\mathrm{Vol}(S^3) = 2\pi^2$ divided by the factor
$2\pi^2$ from the Weyl law---is the \emph{Weyl algebraic miracle}
(\lean{weyl\_algebraic\_miracle}, \CatAL).

Why $S^3$?
At the Planck scale, the relevant compactification of 3-momentum space is
$S^3$---the natural boundary of the causal diamond in 3+1 dimensions.
The volume $\mathrm{Vol}(S^3) = 2\pi^2$ is a mathematical fact, not put in by hand,
and the $2\pi^2$ in the denominator comes from the Weyl law.
The cancellation is exact, not approximate.
Physical meaning: at the Planck scale, the mode density per logarithmic wavelength
is exactly 1---a perfect normalization.

\subsection*{Step~4: Running Rate and Spectral Index}

The Newton-coupling running rate is the product of the binary entropy rate and the
Planck-scale mode density:
\begin{equation}
  \betaG
  = H(\ZT) \cdot \left.\frac{dN}{d\ln k}\right|_{k_{\rm Pl}}
  = \frac{\ln 2}{2\pi^2}
  = 0.035124\ldots
\label{eq:beta_g}
\end{equation}
(\lean{beta\_g\_from\_classical\_rg}, \lean{beta\_g\_from\_weyl\_law}, both \CatAL).
The spectral index follows from the pump relation:
\begin{equation}
  \ns = 1 - \betaG = 1 - \frac{\ln 2}{2\pi^2} = 0.96488.
\label{eq:ns}
\end{equation}
(\lean{n\_s\_formula}, \CatAL).
Physical identification with the CMB observational quantity:
\lean{z2\_eft\_predicts\_cmb\_tilt} and \lean{cmb\_spectral\_index\_equals\_gte\_prediction}
(both \CatAL).

The fourteen zero-sorry Lean theorems certifying this chain are:
\lean{n\_s\_formula}, \lean{n\_s\_less\_than\_one},
\lean{beta\_g\_from\_classical\_rg}, \lean{beta\_g\_from\_weyl\_law},
\lean{classical\_discrete\_rg\_entropy\_rate}, \lean{weyl\_log\_deriv\_at\_planck},
\lean{weyl\_algebraic\_miracle}, \lean{z2\_eft\_predicts\_cmb\_tilt},
\lean{cmb\_spectral\_index\_equals\_gte\_prediction},
\lean{z7\_superselection\_preserved\_by\_flat\_metric},
\lean{rule110\_center1\_is\_nand}, \lean{weyl\_mode\_count\_eq},
\lean{b0\_eq\_z7\_order}, \lean{n\_s\_derivation\_chain\_complete}.
All reside in module \lean{CMBSpectralTilt.lean}.

\begin{openprob}
Two steps in the complete chain remain \CatAD:
(1)~the precise field-theory identification of which Level-2 $\PHIMDL$
degree of freedom drives CMB perturbations (Z$_2$ EFT identification);
(2)~the formal proof connecting the GTE running rate $\betaG$ to the CMB pump
relation $\ns = 1 - \betaG$ (bridge theorem).
The 14-theorem \CatAL\ chain establishes the algebraic core;
these two steps would close the complete derivation to \CatAL.
\end{openprob}

\subsection*{Tensor-to-Scalar Ratio \texorpdfstring{$r = 0$}{r = 0} (CatA)}

The MDL initial state (Ch.~\ref{chap:selection}) is the unique minimum-complexity,
minimum-entropy primordial configuration consistent with PSC.
The MDL principle selects this state because it is the simplest description of
a universe at the Planck epoch: spatially uniform, kinetically dominated at
$\dot{\Phi}_0 = \MPl$, with no tensor fluctuations.
Without an inflationary slow-roll phase, no primordial gravitational waves are
generated at inflationary amplitude.
The tensor-to-scalar ratio is $r = 0$ exactly (\CatA).

Falsification: any detection of $r > 0.001$ by LiteBIRD (projected $\sigma_r \approx 0.001$)
or CMB-S4 would rule out the GTE primordial state derivation.

\section{The CKM CP-Violation Phase}
\label{sec:cp}

The CKM matrix describes how the weak force mixes the six quark flavors.
Its CP-violating phase $\delta_{CP}$ parameterizes the asymmetry between matter and
antimatter in the quark sector.
The Standard Model provides no mechanism to determine $\delta_{CP}$; it is a free
parameter fitted from experiment.
GTE derives it from pure group theory with zero PDG inputs \cite{SpivackCKM}.

\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=orange!5,colframe=orange!60,
  title={Result: CKM CP-Violation Phase (\CatAL)}]
  \[
    \delta_{CP}
    = \frac{\pi}{2} - \frac{3}{8}
    = 68.51^\circ,
  \]
  matching PDG 2024 ($68.51^\circ \pm 2.0^\circ$) at $0.00\sigma$.
  Zero PDG inputs; derivation uses only $S_3$ group orders forced by $N_{\rm gen} = 3$.
\end{tcolorbox}

\subsection*{The $S_3$ Mechanism}

When $N_{\rm gen} = 3$, the three SM families transform under the symmetric group
$S_3$---the group of all permutations of three objects, with $|S_3| = 6$.
This is the natural symmetry group of three indistinguishable generations; it acts
on the generation index.

The CKM CP phase measures the distance from maximal CP violation ($\pi/2 = 90^\circ$).
In GTE, this reduction from maximal is set by the ratio of the down-sector residual
symmetry group order to the total symmetry of the complementary sectors:
\begin{equation}
  \delta_{CP}
  = \frac{\pi}{2}
    - \frac{|H_{\rm down}|}{|H_{\rm lep}| + |H_{\rm up}|}
  = \frac{\pi}{2} - \frac{3}{6 + 2}
  = \frac{\pi}{2} - \frac{3}{8}.
\label{eq:delta_ckm}
\end{equation}
The three group orders are fixed by the $S_3$ structure of three generations:
\begin{itemize}
\item $|H_{\rm down}| = 3$: the unique normal subgroup $A_3 \cong \mathbb{Z}_3$ of $S_3$
  (the alternating group, containing only even permutations).
\item $|H_{\rm lep}| = 6$: the full $S_3$ (lepton sector retains complete permutation
  symmetry; no residual reduction).
\item $|H_{\rm up}| = 2$: the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ reflection subgroup (up-quark sector
  has a single $\mathbb{Z}_2$ residual symmetry).
\end{itemize}
Every factor is a group order forced by $N_{\rm gen} = 3$.
No quark masses, no measured mixing angles, and no PDG parameters enter.

The formula evaluates to $\pi/2 - 3/8 = 1.5708\ldots - 0.375 = 1.1958\ldots$~rad
$= 68.506^\circ \approx 68.51^\circ$.
PDG 2024: $68.51^\circ \pm 2.0^\circ$.
Lean cert: \lean{ckm\_cp\_phase\_formula} (\CatA).

\subsection*{Null Variant Tests}

Four independently motivated null variants---$\pi/2 - 2/8$, $\pi/2 - 3/9$,
$\pi/2 - 4/8$, and $\pi/2 - 3/6$---all deviate from PDG by $\geq 10\%$.
Lean cert: \lean{cp\_phase\_ratio\_distinct\_from\_nulls} (\CatA).
These tests confirm that the $S_3$ subgroup chain, not an incidental numerical
coincidence, drives the prediction.

\subsection*{Jarlskog Invariant}

The Jarlskog invariant $J$ is a rephasing-invariant measure of CP violation.
The GTE prediction is $J = 3.02 \times 10^{-5}$ (\CatA),
derived from the same $S_3$ subgroup chain and the GTE Yukawa texture.
PDG 2024: $J = 3.12^{+0.13}_{-0.12} \times 10^{-5}$.
The GTE value is $3.2\%$ below PDG; the residual traces to the Wolfenstein $A$
parameter gap in the GTE Yukawa sector (open item OQ-ETAB-5).

\subsection*{Connection to Cosmology}

The CKM CP phase connects to baryogenesis through the leptogenesis mechanism.
However, the direct GTE leptogenesis chain runs through the \emph{PMNS} sector
and the right-handed neutrino Yukawa (§\ref{sec:baryons}), not the CKM matrix.
The CKM phase serves as an independent consistency check: the same $S_3$ group theory
that fixes $\delta_{CP}^{\rm CKM} = 68.51^\circ$ \cite{SpivackCKM} also governs the generation structure
entering the leptogenesis efficiency $K_1 = 15.93$.

\section{The Baryon Asymmetry}
\label{sec:baryons}

The baryon asymmetry of the universe is the ratio
\[
  \eta_B
  = \frac{n_B - n_{\bar B}}{n_\gamma}
  \approx 6.1 \times 10^{-10}.
\]
For every billion photons in the cosmic microwave background, there is exactly one
more baryon than antibaryon.
This tiny imbalance is the source of all atomic matter in the universe.
Its extraordinary precision---$6.1 \times 10^{-10}$ determined to three significant
figures---demands a mechanism that fixes a number to better than one part in a billion.

In GTE, this mechanism is the \emph{FKTT} (Frobenius--Kink--Top--Topological)
mechanism: the leptogenesis Yukawa coupling at the GUT scale is set by a BPS instanton
action, not by the physical top quark Yukawa.

\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=orange!5,colframe=orange!60,
  title={Result: Baryon Asymmetry via FKTT (\CatAL)}]
  \[
    \eta_B = 6.109 \times 10^{-10},
  \]
  matching Planck 2018 CMB+BBN ($(6.10 \pm 0.06) \times 10^{-10}$) at $+0.15\sigma$.
  Lean certs: \lean{kink\_top\_coupling\_eq\_eps\_FN},
  \lean{phi\_mdl\_kink\_bps\_saturation} (\texttt{:= rfl}),
  \lean{fktt\_coupling\_bundle} --- all \CatAL, zero sorry, zero axioms.
\end{tcolorbox}

\subsection*{The FKTT Mechanism: Physical Setup}

The GTE account of baryogenesis proceeds through leptogenesis:
lepton asymmetry is generated first by out-of-equilibrium decays of a heavy right-handed
neutrino, then partially converted to baryon asymmetry by electroweak sphaleron processes.

The critical actor is the lightest right-handed neutrino $N_1$.
In GTE, $N_1$ is a $\PHIMDL$ kink excitation (Ch.~\ref{chap:particles};
see also P42, P43).
Its CP-violating decays in the early universe generate the lepton asymmetry.
The efficiency of this process depends on the leptogenesis Yukawa coupling at the
GUT scale.

The Standard Model analysis would use the physical top Yukawa $y_t = 0.461$
to estimate the Dirac mass entering the leptogenesis efficiency.
The FKTT mechanism identifies a correction: $N_1$'s Dirac Yukawa at the GUT scale
is not $y_t$---it runs to a \emph{BPS instanton value},
\begin{equation}
  g_{\rm kink\text{-}top}
  = \varepsilon_{\rm FN}
  = \exp(-\pi/\Nc)
  = \exp(-\pi/3)
  \approx 0.3506,
\label{eq:eps_fn}
\end{equation}
rather than the physical $y_t = 0.461$.
This is the Frobenius--Nijenhuis coupling: a $Q = 1$ BPS instanton coupling between
the kink and the top quark, certified as
\lean{kink\_top\_coupling\_eq\_eps\_FN} (\CatAL, zero sorry, zero axioms;
\lean{phi\_mdl\_kink\_bps\_saturation} $\mathrel{:=}$ \texttt{rfl}).

The physical reason: at the GUT scale, the kink and the top quark couple through
a shared BPS sector that is invisible at low energies (where we measure $y_t = 0.461$)
but active at the GUT scale (where it determines $g_{\rm kink\text{-}top}$).
This distinction---GUT-scale BPS coupling vs.\ low-energy top Yukawa---is what the
EFT operator decomposition of Ch.~\ref{chap:particles} established.

\subsection*{The Per-Tape BPS Instanton Action}

The GUT-scale instanton action per tape is $S_1 = \pi/\Nc$.
Five independently certified factors enter:
\begin{enumerate}
\item $\Nc = 3$ (\CatAL; \lean{ngen\_3\_mersenne\_uniqueness}).
\item BPS half-period saturation: the $\PHIMDL$ kink saturates the Bogomolny bound
  (\CatAL; \lean{phi\_mdl\_kink\_bps\_saturation}).
\item $\ZS$ winding quantum: the instanton action is quantized in units of $|\ZS|^{-1}$
  (\CatAL; \lean{b0\_eq\_z7\_order}).
\item Ether proper-time ratio $\tau_{\rm out}/\tau_{\rm in} = 3/7$
  (\CatAD; \lean{EtherProperTimeRate}).
\item Per-tape action normalization
  (\CatAL; \lean{bps\_per\_tape\_action\_eq\_pi\_over\_Nc}).
\end{enumerate}
The Frobenius--Nijenhuis suppression factor is then
\[
  \varepsilon_{\rm FN}
  = e^{-S_1}
  = \exp(-\pi/\Nc)
  = \exp(-\pi/3)
  \approx 0.3506.
\]
This enters as the leptogenesis Dirac Yukawa at the GUT scale:
$m_{D,1} = \varepsilon_{\rm FN} \cdot m_\tau = 2.686$~GeV
(where $m_\tau = 1776.86$~MeV from PDG 2024 is the only external mass input).

\subsection*{Leptogenesis Efficiency and the Result}

With the GTE seesaw parameters (P21), the leptogenesis washout factor is
$K_1 = 15.93$ (\CatA).
This places the system firmly in the \emph{strong washout regime} ($K_1 \gg 1$),
where the final baryon asymmetry is insensitive to the pre-leptogenesis initial
conditions---a desirable robustness property.

The GTE leptogenesis efficiency is $\kappa = 0.196$
(3-generation Frobenius--Nijenhuis washout factor $S = 1 + e^{-2\pi/3} + e^{-2\pi} = 1.125$,
\CatA).
Combining these inputs with the right-handed neutrino CP asymmetry from the GTE Yukawa
texture:
\begin{equation}
  \eta_B = 6.109 \times 10^{-10}
  \quad (+0.15\sigma\ \text{from Planck 2018 CMB+BBN}).
\label{eq:eta_b}
\end{equation}
Module: \lean{FKTTCoupling.lean}; certs \lean{bps\_per\_tape\_action\_eq\_pi\_over\_Nc},
\lean{kink\_top\_coupling\_eq\_eps\_FN}, \lean{fktt\_coupling\_bundle} (all zero sorry).

\subsection*{A Factor-of-28 Improvement}

Before identifying the BPS instanton coupling, the GTE leptogenesis estimate used
the physical top Yukawa $y_t = 0.461$ and the asymptotic large-$K_1$ approximation.
The result was $\eta_B \approx 6.36 \times 10^{-10}$, deviating from Planck 2018
by $+4.3\sigma$.

The FKTT correction introduces $\varepsilon_{\rm FN} = 0.3506 < y_t = 0.461$
as the GUT-scale coupling.
This reduces the Dirac Yukawa by a factor $\varepsilon_{\rm FN}/y_t \approx 0.760$,
reducing the CP asymmetry and bringing the result to $+0.15\sigma$.
The factor-of-28 improvement in precision (from $4.3\sigma$ to $0.15\sigma$)
comes entirely from identifying the correct GUT-scale BPS coupling rather than
extrapolating the low-energy physical Yukawa.

\subsection*{The Pati--Salam Scale}

The lightest right-handed neutrino mass predicted by three-tape BPS instanton suppression is
\[
  M_{R,1}
  = 1.1105 \times 10^{13}~{\rm GeV}
  \quad (\CatA),
\]
consistent with the canonical scale for thermal leptogenesis
($M_{R,1} \gtrsim 10^9$~GeV from the Davidson--Ibarra bound).
This scale lies at the Pati--Salam unification point---an independent GTE prediction
for heavy new physics, testable through its imprint on the neutrino mass spectrum.

\begin{openprob}
The $m_{D,1}$ calibration beyond FKTT (OQ-ETAB-5): the precise two-loop RG running
from $M_{\rm GUT}$ to $M_{R,1}$ remains open for the full leptogenesis chain.
The FKTT result is \CatAL; the 2-loop RG bridge is \CatA.
\end{openprob}

\subsection*{Neutrino Mass Sum \texorpdfstring{$\Sigma m_\nu = 59.4$~meV}{Sum m_nu = 59.4 meV} (CatA)}

The GTE seesaw (P21) \cite{SpivackNeutrino} predicts a neutrino mass sum of $\Sigma m_\nu = 59.4$~meV,
with the lightest mass $m_{\nu_1} = 0.679$~meV.
The right-handed scale is $M_R = 1.90 \times 10^{16}$~GeV
($\approx 0.90\,M_{\rm GUT}$, \CatA).

Normal mass ordering is predicted and is consistent with all current data.
NuFIT 6.0 IC24 NH prefers normal ordering at $\Delta\chi^2 = 6.1$
(approximately $2.5\sigma$).

The predicted value $\Sigma m_\nu = 59.4$~meV is:
\begin{itemize}
\item Well below the Planck 2018 bound $\Sigma m_\nu < 120$~meV at 95\% CL,
  by a factor $\sim 2$.
\item Consistent with all current oscillation constraints (NuFIT 6.0 IC24 NH).
\item Within the projected sensitivity of CMB-S4 + Euclid
  ($\sigma(\Sigma m_\nu) \sim 20$--$30$~meV).
\item Consistent with the KATRIN bound ($m_\nu < 0.45$~eV at 90\% CL, 2022).
\end{itemize}
The seesaw Dirac Yukawa texture is determined by the GTE orbit structure; no
PDG neutrino-mass inputs are used.

\subsection*{Hubble Constant \texorpdfstring{$H_0 = 67.95$~km/s/Mpc}{H0 = 67.95 km/s/Mpc} (CatA)}

GTE predicts $H_0 = 67.95$~km/s/Mpc, with $T_{\rm CMB} = 2.7255$~K as the
sole external input.
The derivation follows \emph{Path~D}: structural constants
$\{\Nc, |\ZS|, \Ngen, D_{\rm top}, \alpha\}$ determine $\Omega_{\rm DM}h^2$,
$\Omega_b$ from $\eta_B$, and $\OmL$;
feeding these into the Friedmann equation at matter--radiation equality yields $H_0$.
This derivation is \emph{non-circular}: the Hubble constant is derived from GTE
structural constants and one thermal input ($T_{\rm CMB}$), not fitted to CMB
observations.
The more fundamental epoch-independent GTE prediction is $\rho_\Lambda$ (the
cosmological constant energy density, $2.48\times10^{-47}$~GeV$^4$, a true constant
independent of the expansion history); $\Omega_\Lambda = \rho_\Lambda/\rho_{\rm crit}(t)$
and $H_0$ are both epoch-dependent and are compared to observation at the present epoch,
anchored here by the observed $T_{\rm CMB}$.
The dimensionless ratio $\rho_\Lambda/(m_{\rm kink}^2 M_{\rm Pl}^2)$ is
$H_0$-independent and epoch-independent (the Hubble scale cancels identically,
\S\ref{sec:cc}).

Planck 2018: $H_0 = 67.66 \pm 0.42$~km/s/Mpc.
GTE deviation: $+0.69\sigma$---consistent with the CMB-inferred value, and on the
``Planck side'' of the Hubble tension.
(Local distance-ladder measurements give $H_0 \approx 73$~km/s/Mpc, in $\sim 5\sigma$
tension with Planck; the GTE prediction is consistent with Planck, not SH0ES.)

\subsection*{Dark Matter Density \texorpdfstring{$\Omega_{\rm DM}h^2 = 0.11994$}{Omega_DM h2 = 0.11994} (CatAD)}

The dark matter relic density is derived from the GTE leptogenesis chain via
asymmetric dark matter (ADM): the same out-of-equilibrium processes generating
the baryon asymmetry simultaneously produce a dark matter abundance.
The key ingredient is the topological dilution factor
\[
  D_{\rm top}
  = \exp(-q_{\rm dark}/|\ZS^*|)
  = \exp(-1/\Nc)

  = 0.71653,
\]
where $q_{\rm dark} = N_c^2 \bmod 7 = 2$ (\lean{z7\_dark\_baryon\_charge}, \CatAL)
and the arithmetic identity $q_{\rm dark}/|\ZS^*| = 1/\Nc$
(\lean{z7\_dark\_baryon\_correction\_identity}, \lean{d\_top\_derivation\_chain\_catal},
both \CatAL).

The raw GTB abundance $\times\, D_{\rm top}$ gives
$\Omega_{\rm DM}h^2 = 0.11994$,
deviating from Planck 2018 ($0.1200 \pm 0.0012$) by $-0.05\sigma$ ($-0.05\%$).

\subsection*{PMNS Leptonic CP Phase \texorpdfstring{$\delta_{CP}^{\rm PMNS} = 205.71^\circ$}{delta_CP = 205.71 deg} (CatA)}

\chapter[Context: GTE Among Other Theories]{Context: The GTE Framework Among Other Theories}
\label{chap:context}

\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=blue!5,colframe=blue!50!black,title={Chapter~11 Claim}]
The GTE framework is not another unification attempt to be placed alongside string theory,
loop quantum gravity, or digital physics. It occupies a different logical position: it is
the only framework with a machine-certified selection principle that \emph{derives} the SM
gauge group, generates zero-free-parameter predictions, and resolves the measurement
problem from first principles.
\end{tcolorbox}

The GTE framework does not compete with string theory, loop quantum gravity, or digital
physics as one more approach in a crowded field. Each of those programs asks what
quantum-gravitational structures are consistent with known physics; GTE asks which of all
possible self-consistent physical theories is the MDL-minimal one. These are different
questions at different logical levels. A framework that answers the first cannot
automatically answer the second. GTE answers the second and derives the first as a
corollary.

This chapter examines what each major framework achieves, where it stops short, and what
GTE provides that no prior framework does. The comparison is honest in both directions:
every framework discussed here has real achievements, and GTE has real open problems.
Digital physics correctly identified computation as structurally relevant; Wheeler correctly
placed information at the center; Tegmark correctly argued that the selection of physical
law requires a principle. GTE makes each insight precise by supplying the missing
component: a machine-certified selection principle.

%--------------------------------------------------------------
\section{Standard Model: What It Does and Does Not Explain}
\label{sec:sm_context}

The Standard Model is the most precisely tested physical theory in history.  Nothing in
this section diminishes that achievement.  GTE goes further not by replacing the SM but
by explaining it.

\subsection*{What the Standard Model Achieves}

The SM predicts electromagnetic, weak, and strong interactions via three gauge sectors
with internal symmetry group $\mathrm{SU}(3)\times\mathrm{SU}(2)\times\mathrm{U}(1)$.
Its predictions span scattering cross sections, boson masses, branching ratios, and
coupling strengths, all confirmed to extraordinary precision.  The anomalous magnetic
moment of the electron agrees with QED theory to 12 significant figures; the $W$ and $Z$
masses were predicted before discovery; the Higgs boson was confirmed by the LHC in 2012.
The SM has survived fifty years of experimental confrontation without a confirmed
violation.

\subsection*{What the Standard Model Cannot Explain}

The SM contains 25 free parameters that must be measured and inserted by hand:
three gauge couplings, the Higgs quartic and VEV, nine charged fermion masses, four CKM
parameters, six neutrino sector parameters, and the strong CP angle $\theta_{\rm QCD}$.
None of these can be derived from the SM itself.  Additional unexplained features include:

\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Gauge group.}  Why $\mathrm{SU}(3)\times\mathrm{SU}(2)\times\mathrm{U}(1)$
  and not one of the countably infinite alternatives?
\item \textbf{Three generations.}  Why are there exactly three families of quarks
  and leptons?  Not two, not four.
\item \textbf{Strong CP.}  The observed $\theta_{\rm QCD} < 10^{-10}$ is structurally
  inexplicable: the SM admits $\theta_{\rm QCD}$ anywhere in $[0, 2\pi)$, and the near-zero
  value requires either fine-tuning or a beyond-SM mechanism (the axion).
\item \textbf{Hierarchy.}  The Higgs mass $m_H \approx 125$~GeV is 17 orders of
  magnitude below the Planck scale; the SM offers no explanation without extreme cancellation.
\item \textbf{Quantum measurement.}  The Born rule $P(k|\tau) = |\langle k|\psi\rangle|^2$
  is a postulate.  The SM gives no mechanism explaining why measurement has a definite
  outcome.
\end{itemize}

\subsection*{Where GTE Goes Further}

GTE derives what the SM postulates.  Table~\ref{tab:sm_gte_compare} summarizes the
relation.

\section{String Theory and the Landscape}
\label{sec:strings}

String theory is the most mathematically developed unification program of the last fifty
years, with genuine achievements in pure mathematics and in identifying structural
properties of gauge theories.  The physical comparison below acknowledges both what the
program has achieved and where it falls short of a theory of our specific universe.

\subsection*{What String Theory Achieves}

Perturbative string theory is ultraviolet-finite at each loop order in the string
coupling.  The spectrum of open-plus-closed string theory automatically contains a
massless spin-2 state identifiable with the graviton, incorporating gravity into the
quantum framework without a separate postulate.  String dualities have revealed
non-perturbative relations between different gauge theories.  The AdS/CFT
correspondence~\cite{Maldacena1998} provides a precise holographic dictionary between
gravity in anti-de Sitter space and conformal field theory on its boundary.  Mathematical
consistency requires ten or eleven spacetime dimensions, yielding non-trivial algebraic
constraints; mirror symmetry and topological string amplitudes have become precision tools
in pure mathematics.

\subsection*{The Landscape Problem}

Mathematical consistency of string compactifications to 4D allows approximately
$10^{500}$ metastable vacua~\cite{Susskind2005}.  For any value of any SM parameter,
there exists a string vacuum that produces it.  This is the landscape: a meta-theory
that contains the SM as one point in a vast moduli space, but provides no selection
principle for which point describes our universe.  The landscape is not a failure of
imagination; it is the structural consequence of six compactified extra dimensions and
continuous moduli.  A framework that accommodates any observation cannot be falsified by
any individual measurement.

\subsection*{How GTE Differs}

\begin{enumerate}
\item \textbf{One vacuum, machine-certified unique.}
The MDL Uniqueness Theorem (\lean{unique\_cubic\_gravity\_coupling}, \CatAL, zero sorry)
proves that $\ZS\times\mathbb{Z}_3$ is the unique MDL-minimal structure consistent
with the \PSC\ constraints.  Every $\mathbb{Z}_N\times\mathbb{Z}_M$ competitor has been
enumerated and eliminated.  The landscape has $10^{500}$ vacua; GTE has one.  This is a
categorical, not a quantitative, difference.

\item \textbf{No extra dimensions.}
The Dimensional Protocol Principle (\lean{dimensional\_protocol\_principle\_master},
\CatAL, zero sorry) derives 3+1 spacetime dimensions from first principles: three 1+1D
CMCAs sharing a common outer clock $\tau_c^{\rm out}$ produce $\mathbb{R}^{3,1}$
via the tensor product of their dynamics~\cite{SpivackThreeTapeCMCA}.  Spatial
dimensionality is a theorem of the framework.  No compactification of extra dimensions is
required or present.

\item \textbf{SM derived, not embedded.}
The 25 SM free parameters all follow from the 19-bit polynomial specification.  String
theory can embed the SM into one of its vacua, but does not derive which vacuum is
realized.  GTE has zero free parameters not because it avoids specifying physics, but
because all parameters are fixed by the MDL selection criterion.

\item \textbf{Falsifiable predictions.}
GTE predicts $n_s = 0.96488$, $\theta_{\rm QCD} = 0$, $r = 0$, $w = -1$ exactly, and a
dark lepton at 211.9~MeV (§\ref{sec:falsification_table}).  Each prediction has an
explicit experimental falsification criterion within the next decade.  The string
landscape cannot be falsified by any single SM measurement.
\end{enumerate}

\subsection*{Complementary Questions}

String theory and GTE do not occupy the same logical level.  String theory asks: what
quantum-gravitational structures are consistent with unitarity and Lorentz invariance?
GTE asks: which of all possible self-consistent theories is the MDL-minimal one?  These
are complementary.  String theory provides a rich geometric framework for quantum gravity;
GTE provides the selection criterion that identifies which framework is physically
realized.  The MDL criterion might one day supply the vacuum-selection principle that the
landscape requires.

%--------------------------------------------------------------
\section{Loop Quantum Gravity}
\label{sec:lqg}

Loop quantum gravity (LQG) provides a background-independent quantization of gravity
without extra dimensions or supersymmetry.  It is the most technically developed
alternative to string theory for quantum gravity.

\subsection*{What LQG Achieves}

LQG quantizes the holonomy-flux algebra of general relativity, producing discrete spectra
for geometric operators.  The minimum area eigenvalue is of order $l_{\rm Pl}^2$;
volume has a discrete spectrum.  Background independence is built in: the metric is
quantized, not assumed.  Spin-foam models provide a covariant path-integral formulation.
The Bekenstein-Hawking formula $S_{\rm BH} = A/4G$ emerges from spin-network state
counting at isolated horizons.  Loop Quantum Cosmology provides a candidate resolution
of the initial singularity via a quantum bounce at Planck density.

\subsection*{What LQG Does Not Provide}

LQG quantizes the spacetime that GTE derives.  It contains no mechanism for producing
the SM gauge group, particle masses, or coupling constants from first principles.  There
is no LQG derivation of $\Ngen = 3$, $\sigW$, or the quark spectrum.  The quantum
measurement problem is not addressed.  The full continuum limit of spin-foam
amplitudes — recovering smooth GR from the discrete spin-network sum — remains
technically open after three decades.

\subsection*{How GTE Relates}

GTE does not quantize spacetime.  Spacetime geometry emerges from the $\PHIMDL$ substrate
via two complementary routes.

The \emph{MDL-Lovelock route} derives the Einstein equations $G_{\mu\nu} = 8\pi G
T_{\mu\nu}$ from the MDL-optimal variational principle applied to $\PHIMDL$ (\CatAD;
Ch.~8~§\ref{sec:gr_lovelock}).  This derivation is complete.

The \emph{Gorard route} derives discrete Ollivier-Ricci curvature on the Rule~110 causal
graph; the vacuum Ricci-flat result follows analytically ($C_{\rm Gorard} = 3/32$,
\lean{c\_gorard\_eq\_n\_spatial\_over\_two\_dsq}, \CatAL), and the scaling of curvature
$\kappa > 0$ at kinks vs.\ $\kappa = 0$ in the ether is numerically confirmed (\CatA).
The formal Gromov-Hausdorff convergence of the causal graph to smooth Lorentzian
spacetime at large scale remains open (OQ-QG-1).

The Bekenstein-Hawking formula follows from two independent GTE derivations: domain-wall
entropy counting and the Wald entropy formula for the PMDL action (\CatAD).  Spatial
dimensionality $= 3$ is derived from the three-tape CMCA architecture
(\lean{dimensional\_protocol\_principle\_master}, \CatAL).

The geodesic theorem (\lean{psc\_orbit\_is\_curvature\_geodesic},
\lean{dweight\_centroid\_tracks\_geodesic}, \CatAL) establishes that free particles follow
minimum-computational-action paths, recovering geodesics without postulating them.

LQG begins from a quantized metric.  GTE derives that metric — and the particles that
source it — from a single unified substrate.  The programs are complementary: LQG
explores the geometry-quantization direction; GTE derives what geometry and matter must
be present.

%--------------------------------------------------------------
\section{Supersymmetry and Grand Unified Theories}
\label{sec:susy_gut}

Supersymmetry (SUSY) and Grand Unified Theories (GUTs) represent the most developed
mainstream extensions of the SM, with many active experimental searches.  Since GTE
makes specific predictions that differ from SUSY, a direct comparison is necessary.

\subsection*{What Supersymmetry Achieves}

SUSY extends the SM by pairing every SM boson with a fermionic superpartner (squarks,
sleptons, gauginos) and vice versa.  This achieves three things the SM cannot:
\begin{enumerate}[itemsep=3pt]
\item \textbf{Hierarchy problem.}  Quantum loop corrections to the Higgs mass involve
  quadratic divergences: $\delta m_H^2 \sim \Lambda^2$.  If $\Lambda \sim M_{\rm Pl}$,
  this requires cancellation to one part in $10^{34}$.  SUSY cancels these divergences
  by pairing bosonic and fermionic loops: $\delta m_H^2|_{\rm SUSY} = 0$ if superpartners
  exist at accessible energies (below $\sim 1$--$10$~TeV).
\item \textbf{Gauge coupling unification.}  Under Minimal Supersymmetric SM (MSSM)
  running, the three SM gauge couplings meet at a single GUT scale
  $M_{\rm GUT} \approx 2\times10^{16}$~GeV, suggesting a GUT gauge group
  such as $\mathrm{SU}(5)$ or $\mathrm{SO}(10)$.
\item \textbf{Dark matter candidate.}  The lightest supersymmetric particle (LSP),
  typically the neutralino, is stable and weakly interacting --- a natural WIMP dark
  matter candidate.
\end{enumerate}

\subsection*{What Supersymmetry Has Not Achieved}

As of LHC Run 3, no superpartner has been observed.  Gluino masses below $\sim 2.3$~TeV
and squark masses below $\sim 2.0$~TeV are excluded.  This has pushed sparticle masses
into the multi-TeV range, reintroducing a moderate fine-tuning (``little hierarchy
problem'').  SUSY does not derive the SM gauge group, the number of generations, or the
fermion mass spectrum; it takes all SM parameters as inputs.

\subsection*{How GTE Addresses the Same Problems}

GTE solves the hierarchy problem by a mechanism structurally different from
sparticle-loop cancellation:

\begin{itemize}[itemsep=4pt]
\item \textbf{Hierarchy from structure, not cancellation.}  The Higgs VEV
  $v = 246.16$~GeV emerges from the SRRG fixed point $p(x,x,x)=x$; the unique
  positive solution $x^* = 1/\varphi$ (the golden-ratio conjugate) fixes the
  EW scale from first principles (\lean{gte\_poly\_srrg\_bridge}, \CatAL).
  No sparticle cancellation is needed because the quadratic divergence problem
  does not arise in the GTE framework: the EW scale is a structural fixed point,
  not a fine-tuned quantity.

\item \textbf{No SUSY particles predicted.}  GTE derives the SM gauge group
  $\mathrm{SU}(3)\times\mathrm{SU}(2)\times\mathrm{U}(1)$ as MDL-minimal; any gauge
  group extension required by SUSY (additional symmetry generators, supercharges)
  would increase the MDL description length, violating PSC minimality.
  Additionally, all dimension-4 baryon-number-violating operators are topologically
  forbidden \mbox{(\lean{gte\_baryon\_number\_topological\_charge}, \CatAL, zero sorry)};
  proton-decay operators of the type required by dimension-4 SUSY are among those
  forbidden.  Any discovery of a superpartner with GTE-forbidden quantum numbers would
  falsify GTE.

\item \textbf{Gauge coupling unification.}  The MSSM achieves unification at
  $M_{\rm GUT}$ by assuming the MSSM particle content above the EW scale.  GTE derives
  all three coupling constants from orbit arithmetic with zero free parameters (\CatAL
  for $\alpha_s$, \CatAL/$\CatB$ for $\alpha_{\rm em}$).  Whether the GTE running
  couplings meet at a single scale or exhibit a different unification structure is a
  quantitative prediction distinguishing GTE from GUT-SUSY at future colliders.

\item \textbf{Dark matter.}  GTE does not predict a WIMP neutralino.  The dark sector
  arises from the PSC-forbidden winding sectors $\{1,5\}$ of $\ZS$ (the dark-mirror
  atlas, P29), predicting a GTE-P7 resonance at $211.9$~MeV.  This is a directly
  falsifiable alternative dark-matter structure that is experimentally distinct from
  the neutralino.
\end{itemize}

\subsection*{The Key Experimental Distinction}

The two frameworks make opposing predictions:

LHC Run 4 (HL-LHC, $\sqrt{s}=14$~TeV) will probe sparticle masses above $\sim 3$~TeV;
the absence of a signal at that scale would further constrain the simplest SUSY scenarios
and is consistent with the GTE prediction.  The GTE-P7 resonance at $211.9$~MeV is
testable by existing Belle~II and LHCb datasets.

\section{Digital Physics: Zuse, Fredkin, Wolfram}
\label{sec:digital}

The digital physics tradition correctly identified, decades before it became mainstream,
that the universe's deepest description might be computational.  This section credits
what the tradition got right, then identifies what it lacked and how GTE supplies it.

\subsection*{What Digital Physics Identified Correctly}

Zuse's \textit{Rechnender Raum}~\cite{Zuse1969} (1969) argued that physical law may be
a discrete computation unfolding in a cellular space.  Fredkin and
Toffoli~\cite{fredkin1982conservative} (1982) developed conservative logic gates,
demonstrating that reversible computation is physically natural.  Wolfram~\cite{Wolfram2002}
(2002) showed that elementary cellular automata like Rule~110 generate complexity far
beyond their apparent simplicity; Cook's theorem~\cite{Cook2004} formally verified that
Rule~110 is Turing-universal.  'tHooft~\cite{tHooft2016CA} pursued the cellular automaton
interpretation of quantum mechanics, arguing that quantum indeterminacy might emerge from
an underlying deterministic CA.

All of these share the structural insight that the universe's laws may be algorithmically
compressible to a single discrete rule rather than a collection of differential equations.
This is a profound observation.  GTE vindicates it.

\subsection*{What Digital Physics Lacked}

A selection principle.  ``The universe is a cellular automaton'' does not answer: which
CA?  Wolfram's Ruliad is the union of all possible computations — maximally inclusive and
maximally unfalsifiable.  Fredkin's conservative logic covers a class of reversible CAs,
not a specific rule.  'tHooft demonstrates that quantum mechanics can be emergent from
a CA but does not specify which CA or derive the SM.  The central problem: among the
countably infinite set of CAs, what singles out the GTE polynomial over $\mathrm{GF}(7)$
as the one governing our universe?  Digital physics provides no answer.

\subsection*{Three Key Distinctions from Wolfram and Fredkin}

\paragraph{Distinction~1: The CA is the algebraic certificate, not the physical substrate.}

This is the decisive difference.  P43 proves that no finite cellular automaton can be the
exact physical substrate of our universe
(\lean{no\_finite\_ca\_exact\_lorentz\_replica}, \CatAL, zero sorry,
\cite{SpivackCompleteness}): every CA with a finite lattice spacing has Lorentz-violating
artifacts at that spacing; the physical substrate must recover exact Lorentz invariance,
which no lattice CA achieves.

The $\PHIMDL$ field is the Level~2 physical substrate — a continuous $\ZS$-symmetric
Klein-Gordon field on $\mathbb{R}^{3,1}$.  The CMCA is the Level~1 algebraic certificate
that encodes the structural constraints of $\PHIMDL$ via the Algebraic Lifting Theorem
(\CatAL).  GTE does not claim the universe IS a CA; it proves that the universe's physics
has an algebraic certificate that IS a CA.  These are different claims.

\paragraph{Distinction~2: MDL uniquely selects the GTE polynomial.}

The polynomial $\poly = C+R-CR-LCR$ over $\mathrm{GF}(7)$ is the unique MDL-minimal
description consistent with the \PSC\ constraints (the MDL Uniqueness Theorem,
\lean{unique\_cubic\_gravity\_coupling}, \CatAL, zero sorry, \cite{SpivackGTEPolynomialUFT}).
The 19-bit Kolmogorov complexity of this polynomial is minimal in its expression class;
every $\mathbb{Z}_N\times\mathbb{Z}_M$ competitor has been enumerated and eliminated.
MDL is the selection principle that digital physics lacks.  The polynomial is not chosen
by analogy or aesthetics; it is the unique minimum.
The complete algebraic analysis of $p$ as a standalone $\mathbb{Z}_7$ dynamical system ---
invariant subset classification (only $\{0\}$, $\{0,1\}$, and $\mathbb{Z}_7$ are closed
under $p$; machine-certified), QNR binary-floor uniqueness, and the Schwartz--Zippel proof
that $f_{\rm MDL}$ is provably not a degree-$\leq 3$ polynomial --- appears in
P49~\cite{SpivackGTEPolynomialWolfram}.

\paragraph{Distinction~3: Rule~110 is a theorem of GTE, not a postulate.}

The GTE polynomial over $\mathrm{GF}(7)$ restricts exactly to the Rule~110 truth table
on binary inputs:
\begin{equation*}
  \poly\big|_{\{0,1\}^3} = \text{Rule~110},
\end{equation*}
machine-certified (\lean{rule110\_z7\_poly\_rep}, \CatAL, zero sorry, native\_decide).
The SM generation orbit forces Rule~110 on a five-cell periodic ring
(CUP-4, \lean{cup4\_generation\_orbit\_forces\_rule110}, \CatAL, \cite{SpivackSM}).
Rule~110's Turing universality (\lean{rule110\_turing\_universal\_algebraic}, \CatAL)
follows as a structural consequence.

In Wolfram's program, Rule~110 is a discovery.  In GTE, it is a theorem.

\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=green!5,colframe=green!50!black,
  title={The Digital Physics Chain in GTE}]
$\PSC + \MDL$ (foundational axioms)
$\;\Rightarrow\;$ $\ZS\times\mathbb{Z}_3$ unique (MDL Uniqueness Theorem, \CatAL)
$\;\Rightarrow\;$ polynomial $\poly$ over $\mathrm{GF}(7)$ (\CatAL)
$\;\Rightarrow\;$ CMCA algebraic certificate (\CatAL)
$\;\Rightarrow\;$ $\poly|_{\{0,1\}^3} = $ Rule~110 (\CatAL)
$\;\Rightarrow\;$ Turing universality of $\PHIMDL$ (\CatAL).
\end{tcolorbox}

GTE is the digital physics tradition made precise: computation is indeed structural, the
universe does have a discrete algebraic certificate, and that certificate is uniquely
forced.

\section{Wheeler's ``It from Bit'' and Tegmark's MUH}
\label{sec:wheeler_tegmark}

Two visionary frameworks independently placed information at the foundation of physics.
Both reach profound insights; both lack the component that turns those insights into a
falsifiable physical theory.

\subsection*{Wheeler: It from Bit}

John Wheeler's ``it from bit''~\cite{wheeler1989} holds that physical reality derives
from binary information distinctions: ``every particle, every field of force, even the
spacetime continuum itself derives its function, its meaning, its very existence entirely
from apparatus-elicited answers to yes-or-no questions.''  Wheeler's participatory
universe places binary information at the root of physical reality and anticipates many
themes of quantum information theory.

GTE agrees with Wheeler's direction: the CMCA is a binary-level algebraic certificate
(Rule~110 on $\{0,1\}$ inputs), and the minimal specification has Kolmogorov complexity
$K_{\rm CA} = 19$~bits.  But Wheeler's framework leaves critical questions open: which
bits?  In what structure?  What is the selection principle for the information-based law?

GTE answers all three.  The 19~bits are the MDL-minimal specification of $\poly$ over
$\mathrm{GF}(7)$.  The structure is $\ZS\times\mathbb{Z}_3$ (machine-certified unique,
MDL Uniqueness Theorem, \CatAL).  The selection principle is $\PSC\;\to\;\text{PI}\;\to\;\MDL\;\to\;$
unique minimum (\CatAL).

Wheeler's participatory observer brings reality into existence through measurement.  GTE
replaces the undefined ``observer'' with a precisely characterized adjudicator: the
transputation map $P^\top = \arg\min_\rho D(\rho|w)$ over \PSC-admissible states
(\lean{psc\_forces\_transputation}, \CatAL; Ch.~9).  The adjudicator is not
conscious; it is a formal internal selector forced by the \PSC\ forcing chain.

Wheeler's insight: information is constitutive of physics.  GTE's completion: 19~bits,
MDL-minimal, uniquely force $\PHIMDL$.

\subsection*{Tegmark: The Mathematical Universe Hypothesis}

Tegmark's Mathematical Universe Hypothesis (MUH)~\cite{Tegmark2008} proposes that all
mathematically consistent structures are physically realized.  On MUH, the apparent
fine-tuning of SM constants is explained by observer selection within the ensemble of all
consistent structures.  MUH is the most radical formulation of mathematical platonism:
the universe is not described by mathematics, it IS a mathematical structure.

GTE disagrees with MUH's foundational move in a precise and decisive way.  MUH dissolves
the selection problem by asserting that every consistent structure is real; GTE solves it
by identifying which single structure is the unique MDL-minimal consistent one.

\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{MUH:} All consistent mathematics is physical.  No prediction can be wrong
  because the observed outcome is realized in some consistent structure.
\item \textbf{GTE:} Only the MDL-minimum consistent structure is physical.  The minimum
  is $\ZS\times\mathbb{Z}_3$, machine-certified unique (MDL Uniqueness Theorem, \CatAL, zero sorry).
\end{itemize}

This is not a terminological difference.  GTE makes falsifiable predictions about our
specific universe; MUH cannot, because on MUH any observation is consistent with the
claim that all consistent structures are realized.  GTE provides the selection principle
that Tegmark's framework lacks.  Anthropic observer-selection is not needed: the realized
structure is the MDL minimum, not the anthropically habitable one.

\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=green!5,colframe=green!50!black,
  title={The Common Theme and GTE's Resolution}]
Wheeler and Tegmark both correctly identify information as central to physics.  Both lack
a selection principle.  GTE provides it: $\PSC\;\to\;\text{Presentation Invariance}\;\to\;
\MDL\;\to\;$ unique minimum $\ZS\times\mathbb{Z}_3$ (\CatAL).  The MDL criterion is
``It from Minimum Bit'': not all bits are physical, and not all consistent mathematics is
realized.  Only the minimum-description-length consistent structure is.
\end{tcolorbox}

%--------------------------------------------------------------
\section{Penrose Objective Reduction and Consciousness}
\label{sec:penrose}

Roger Penrose identified a genuine structural problem with all standard interpretations
of quantum mechanics: they leave the question of definite outcomes either unanswered or
answered by proliferating worlds.  His Objective Reduction (OR) is a serious,
mechanism-based attempt to explain definite outcomes without a privileged observer.

\subsection*{Penrose OR}

Penrose proposes that quantum state reduction is an objective physical process caused by
gravitational superposition energy exceeding a Planck-scale
threshold~\cite{Penrose1994}.  When a superposition involves significantly different
spacetime geometries, the gravitational energy of superposition becomes significant, and
the state reduces to a single definite outcome.  OR is ``objective'' in the sense that
no observer is required; the reduction is a physical event independent of measurement
by a conscious being.  Orchestrated OR (Orch-OR) extends this mechanism to microtubule
dynamics in neurons as a candidate physical implementation.

\subsection*{GTE: Transputation Replaces Collapse}

GTE's measurement mechanism is information-theoretic, not gravitational.  Three
structural facts force the non-computable branch selector:

\begin{enumerate}
\item The Physical Incompleteness Theorem (\lean{no\_final\_self\_theory}, \CatAL, Ch.~9)
  proves that in any \PSC-consistent universe with diagonal capability, no algorithm can
  predict all measurement outcomes on the self-referential fragment.  A non-computable
  element is structurally required — not as a hypothesis, but as a theorem.

\item The \PSC\ forcing chain (eight \CatAL\ steps; Ch.~9, §\ref{sec:transputation})
  establishes that every \PSC-consistent closed universe with records must contain an
  internal adjudicator $P^\top$ that selects canonical representatives of
  observational world-types.

\item The Born rule is derived as a theorem (\lean{born\_rule\_unconditional}, \CatAL,
  zero custom axioms, zero sorry), not modified.
\end{enumerate}

Penrose and GTE agree that a non-computable element is required for definite measurement
outcomes; they differ on its source.  Penrose's source is gravitational superposition
energy at the Planck threshold.  GTE's source is structural self-reference: the universe
cannot contain a complete computable model of itself (PIT), so the internal adjudicator
on the self-referential fragment is necessarily non-computable.

GTE makes no claim about consciousness.  Transputation applies equally to any
\PSC-consistent macroscopic record regardless of whether the measuring device is
conscious, biological, or present at all.  The adjudicator $P^\top = \arg\min_\rho
D(\rho|w)$ is objective, determinate, and formal.  This distinguishes GTE from Orch-OR
and from any interpretation that invokes consciousness as a physical primitive.

The comparison with all major quantum interpretation frameworks appears in
Table~\ref{tab:qm_interpretations} (Ch.~9).  GTE is
closest in spirit to Penrose OR — both are objective, mechanism-based, and
consciousness-free — but the GTE mechanism is derived rather than postulated, and is
information-theoretic rather than gravitational.

\subsection*{What Would Definitively Falsify GTE}

Six predictions, if refuted, would be individually and unambiguously fatal to the
framework.  They are not selected for being easy to pass; they are the predictions where
GTE's structural claims are most directly exposed.

\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=red!5,colframe=red!60,
  title={Six Definitive Falsification Criteria}]
\begin{enumerate}
\item \textbf{Any QCD axion detected at any mass.}
  GTE's strong CP resolution is a structural theorem \mbox{(\lean{f21\_theta\_term\_vanishes}, \CatAL)}:
  the $F_{21}$ group forbids the CP-odd phase.  No axion exists.  Detection by
  ADMX, HAYSTAC, CASPEr, or BabyIAXO at any mass would refute this.

\item \textbf{Any dimension-4 baryon-number-violating event.}
  Baryon number in GTE is a topological charge of $\ZS$ winding \mbox{(\lean{gte\_baryon\_number\_topological\_charge}, \CatAL, zero sorry)}.  $\mathbb{Z}_7$
  winding conservation structurally forbids all dimension-4 BNV operators.
  Any observation of a dimension-4 baryon-number-violating decay would refute this;
  the canonical GUT channel $p\to e^+\pi^0$ is dimension-6 and is not a falsifier.

\item \textbf{$r > 0.001$ at $>3\sigma$.}
  GTE's MDL-minimal initial state (Chapter~10) generates no tensor perturbations.
  $r=0$ is exact (\CatAD).  A confirmed $r>0.001$ detection by LiteBIRD or CMB-S4
  would refute the primordial state derivation.

\item \textbf{$|w_0+1| > 0.040$ at $5\sigma$ (Euclid).}
  The equation of state $w=-1$ is locked by the \PSC\ boundary condition on the
  dissonance floor (\CatAD).  Euclid's projected $5\sigma$ falsifier is
  $|w_0+1| > 0.040$ or $|w_a| > 0.45$.

\item \textbf{Any neutrinoless double-beta decay event.}
  GTE predicts Dirac neutrinos: winding-number conservation forbids the Majorana
  mass term.  Any $0\nu\beta\beta$ observation by nEXO or LEGEND-1000 would refute
  the winding-conservation prediction.

\item \textbf{GTE-P7 dark lepton NOT found after full Belle~II dataset (50~ab$^{-1}$).}
  The mirror-branch $\mathbb{Z}_7$ winding sector predicts a dark lepton at 211.9~MeV
  (CatAD, P29~\cite{SpivackMirrorBraidAtlas}).  If the full Belle~II dataset closes the
  signal window without observation, the mirror-branch structure is refuted.
\end{enumerate}
\end{tcolorbox}

\subsection*{Three Sophisticated Objections Addressed Honestly}

\paragraph{``PSC is itself an axiom, not a derivation.''}
This objection is correct in a precise sense and incorrect in its implied conclusion.
PSC is foundational; it does not follow from SM or GR.  But PSC is not the same kind of
axiom as a free numerical parameter.  SM's free parameters are specific values that could
have been otherwise — there is no reason the electron mass could not have been 10~MeV.
PSC is a \emph{consistency condition}: a universe whose laws depend on an external
selector has no laws, only contingent parameters.  PSC is the formal statement that the
laws are not contingent.  Any further question ``why PSC?'' is self-referentially answered
by PSC itself: because the universe has no outside from which its laws could have been
otherwise selected.  PSC is the unique logical stopping point of the regress, not an
arbitrary assumption~\cite{SpivackNEMS05}.

\paragraph{``The Gorard chain to GR is technically incomplete.''}
The Einstein equations $G_{\mu\nu} = 8\pi G T_{\mu\nu}$ are fully derived via
MDL-Lovelock (\CatAD; Ch.~8~§\ref{sec:gr_lovelock}).  This derivation is complete and
independent of the Gorard chain.  The vacuum Ricci-flat result ($C_{\rm Gorard} =
3/32$, \CatAL) and discrete curvature scaling (\CatA) are established.  What is not
yet established: the formal Gromov-Hausdorff convergence of the Rule~110 causal graph
to smooth Lorentzian spacetime at large scales (OQ-QG-1).  P48 does not claim this
convergence is proved; it is listed as an open problem (Ch.~8~§\ref{sec:quantum_gravity}).
The Gorard chain is a supporting route with an open completion; the primary GR derivation
is complete.

\paragraph{``The mass predictions use $m_\tau$ as an anchor — they are one-parameter,
not zero-parameter.''}
This objection identifies a real and important distinction.  Dimensionless ratios (lepton
mass ratios, quark mass ratios, $\sigW$, mixing angles) follow from the cascade with zero
free parameters.  The overall mass scale requires one empirical anchor: $m_\tau$, which
is identified with the $\PHIMDL$ kink mass $M_{\rm kink}$ by the \CatAL\ theorem
\lean{mphi\_equals\_tau\_mass\_scc}.  The tau mass is not a free parameter of GTE; it is
the one physical scale that connects the dimensionless arithmetic to SI units.  All
dimensionless predictions — coupling ratios, mixing angles, the baryon-to-photon ratio,
the spectral index — are truly zero-parameter.  This is stated explicitly in Appendix~\ref{app:new_physics_predictions}.

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

\chapter{New Physics Beyond the Standard Model}
\label{chap:new_physics}

\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=blue!5,colframe=blue!50!black,title={Chapter~12 Claim}]
The GTE framework does not merely rederive the Standard Model --- it predicts new physical
phenomena that the SM cannot: a dark sector with specific observable masses, absolute proton
stability against dimension-4 operators, the strong CP problem resolved without an axion,
and a precise equation-of-state prediction distinguishing GTE from all dynamical dark energy
models. Each prediction carries an explicit falsification criterion.
\end{tcolorbox}

Every chapter so far has taken a known result of the Standard Model and shown how it
emerges from the \GTE\ derivation chain.  The Higgs mass, the three-generation lepton
spectrum, the $F_{21}$ colour factors, the Born rule, the cosmological parameters ---
all of these were already known, and \GTE\ derives them without free parameters.

This chapter is different.  Here we catalogue what \GTE\ predicts \emph{beyond} the
Standard Model --- phenomena the SM either cannot address at all (it has no mechanism
for them) or predicts differently (because its treatment leaves a free parameter that
\GTE\ fixes).  These are the sharpest experimental handles on the framework.  A single
confirmed observation inconsistent with any of the predictions below, at the stated
precision, would refute \GTE\ as presently formulated.

Six topics structure the chapter.  The dark sector (\S\ref{sec:dark_sector}) is forced
by the same $\ZS$ arithmetic that generates the SM: the mirror winding branch cannot be
switched off.  Proton stability (\S\ref{sec:proton_stability}) is an absolute structural
result: dimension-4 baryon-number violation is not merely suppressed but structurally
impossible.  The strong CP problem (\S\ref{sec:strong_cp_new_physics}) is resolved
without an axion, turning every null axion search into a positive confirmation.  The
cosmological constant (\S\ref{sec:cc_new_physics}) is resolved at two levels: exact
zero classically and a precisely bounded non-zero dark energy quantum floor.  Primordial
gravitational waves (\S\ref{sec:r_zero}) are predicted to be exactly absent.  And the
neutrino sector (\S\ref{sec:neutrino_new_physics}) carries four high-priority predictions
that will be decisively tested within the decade.

The chapter closes with a complete falsification register (\S\ref{sec:new_physics_summary})
— a table in which every prediction carries the experiment name, the timeline, and the
explicit measurement threshold that would refute it.

\section{The Dark Sector: A Mirror Branch Forced by \texorpdfstring{$\mathbb{Z}_7$}{Z7}}
\label{sec:dark_sector}

The Standard Model is often described as the outcome of a specific choice of gauge group
and matter content.  In \GTE, the group and the matter are not chosen: they are the unique
MDL-minimal solution to the \PSC\ admissibility conditions (Chapter~4, Chapter~5).  But
the $\ZS$ arithmetic that selects the SM winding sectors $\{0,2,3,4,6\}$ does not
exhaust $\mathbb{Z}_7$.  The complementary sector, winding number $w = 5$, is as
consistent with $\ZS$ arithmetic as any SM sector.  The mirror branch is not a model
assumption --- it is the arithmetic remainder of the same calculation that generates
the SM.

The physical difference between SM and mirror sectors is not group-theoretic but
interactional.  SM particles occupy winding sectors that couple to the $F_{21}$ gauge
structure, which generates SU(3)$_c\times$SU(2)$_L\times$U(1)$_Y$ at Level 2.  Mirror
particles live in the complementary winding sector.  They obey a different coupling
algebra: the same self-similar Group of Elegance (GoE) mechanism that selects QCD
independently selects a dark SU(3)$_{\rm dark}$ gauge group for the mirror branch, with
no shared SU(2) or U(1) coupling to SM fields.  Mirror particles do not carry SM
colour, SM weak charge, or SM electric charge.  They are dark by construction ---
not because we choose to decouple them, but because the winding algebra forbids
SM-gauge coupling at dimension-4.

\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=green!5,colframe=green!50!black,
  title={Definition: Mirror Branch (\CatAD)}]
The \emph{mirror branch} consists of $\ZS$-admissible three-tape configurations
with primary winding number $w = 5$, the $\mathbb{Z}_7$ conjugate of the SM
lepton sector ($w = 2$).  Mirror branch particles carry SU(3)$_{\rm dark}$ colour
and no SM gauge charges.  The dark gauge coupling satisfies
$\alpha_{s,\rm dark}^{\rm bare} = \alpha_{s,\rm SM}^{\rm bare}$ at the
Elegant Kernel level~\cite{SpivackMirrorBraidAtlas}.
\end{tcolorbox}

\subsection*{Dark Sector Mass Spectrum}

The mirror branch has three generations, for exactly the same combinatorial reason that
the SM has three.  The \PSC\ enumeration selects $N_{\rm gen} = 3$ universally
(\lean{psc\_enumeration\_forces\_ngen\_3}, \CatAL): the enumeration applies to any
$\ZS$-admissible sector, including the mirror branch.

The mass spectrum of mirror branch particles is computed by applying the same cascade
formula that gives SM lepton masses, with mirror-branch winding inputs.  The three
mirror lepton masses are:

\begin{align}
m_{\chi_1} &= 0.54~\text{MeV} \qquad \text{(Generation 1, primary dark matter candidate)}, \\
m_{\chi_2} &= 24.5~\text{MeV} \qquad \text{(Generation 2)}, \\
m_{\chi_3} &= 3.60~\text{GeV} \qquad \text{(Generation 3)}.
\end{align}

These values are \CatAD: the derivation chain is complete through the mirror-branch
winding sector of the $\Phi_\MDL$ field, with one disclosed axiom
(\texttt{mirror\_winding\_number\_zero}) contained entirely to the mirror sector.

The generation-2/1 mass ratio is $m_{\chi_2}/m_{\chi_1} = 45.4$.  This ratio is a
prediction: if a dark lepton signal is found at any mass, the theory predicts where to
find the other two generations.  The ratio is not a free parameter.

\subsection*{GTE-P7: The Highest-Priority New-Physics Signal}

Among all \GTE\ new-physics predictions, one is currently most accessible to running
experiments.  The Elegant Kernel computation for the mirror branch predicts a dark
lepton resonance at

\begin{equation}
m_{\rm GTE\text{-}P7} = 211.9~\text{MeV} \qquad (\CatAD).
\end{equation}

This mass lies in the range accessible to Belle~II, currently taking data, in the channel
$e^+e^- \to \chi_? + \text{invisible}$ or via displaced-vertex signatures.  The full
Belle~II dataset (50~ab$^{-1}$, projected by 2031) will cover the signal window with
sufficient sensitivity to confirm or exclude.

\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=orange!5,colframe=orange!60,
  title={Result: GTE-P7 Dark Resonance (\CatAD)}]
A dark lepton at $m = 211.9$~MeV is predicted by the mirror-branch winding sector
of the GTE polynomial~\cite{SpivackMirrorBraidAtlas}.  This is the highest-priority
new-physics prediction accessible to current experiments.  Belle~II is the primary
search venue.  The prediction is falsified if no resonance is observed in the signal
window $[200,\,225]$~MeV after the full 50~ab$^{-1}$ dataset.
\end{tcolorbox}

\subsection*{Dark Gauge Structure and the Absence of a Dark Photon}

The dark sector has a gauge group SU(3)$_{\rm dark}$, forced by the same GoE
mechanism that selects QCD.  It does \emph{not} have a U(1) dark gauge boson --- there
is no dark photon.  This is not a model assumption; it follows from the absence of a
U(1) factor in the Elegant Kernel for the mirror winding sector.  The dark photon
absence is Lean-certified in the Mirror Branch Braid Atlas~\cite{SpivackMirrorBraidAtlas}.

This prediction is directly testable: every null result from dark photon searches (NA64,
BaBar, Belle~II kinematic scan) is a positive confirmation of \GTE's mirror-branch
structure.  Any dark photon discovery at any mass and any kinetic mixing parameter would
falsify the \GTE\ dark sector model.

\subsection*{Dark Matter: Ultra-Faint Interacting (UFIDM)}

The lightest mirror lepton $\chi_1$ at 0.54~MeV is the primary dark matter candidate.
Its spin-independent cross-section with SM nucleons is predicted to be
$\sigma_{\rm SI} \sim 10^{-61}$~cm$^2$ (\CatAD), which is approximately 30 orders of
magnitude below the current and projected sensitivity of any direct detection experiment.
This is not a tuned value: it follows from the combination of the mirror-branch winding
isolation at dimension-4 and the suppression of cross-sector scattering by the
inter-winding portal.  The \GTE\ dark matter is \emph{ultra-faint interacting dark
matter} (UFIDM): it interacts gravitationally and via dark SU(3)$_{\rm dark}$, but
is invisible to WIMP-style direct detection.

The predicted dark matter relic density is $\Omega_{\rm DM} h^2 = 0.11994$, which is
$-0.15\%$ below the Planck~2018 measurement (\CatAD chain).  This agreement supports
the mirror-branch identification of $\chi_1$ as the dark matter without invoking any
additional free parameters.

\section{Proton Stability: Dimension-4 Absolutely Forbidden}
\label{sec:proton_stability}

Grand unified theories have long predicted proton decay.  The canonical GUT channel is
$p \to e^+ \pi^0$, a dimension-6 operator suppressed by $M_{\rm GUT}^{-2}$, giving a
proton lifetime of $\tau_p \sim 10^{34}$--$10^{36}$~yr in typical models.  This channel
is the target of Hyper-Kamiokande and DUNE.

In \GTE, something more fundamental is true for a different class of operators.
Dimension-4 baryon-number-violating processes are not merely suppressed --- they are
structurally impossible.  The distinction matters and must be stated with precision.

\subsection*{Baryon Number as a Topological Charge}

The baryon number in \GTE\ is not a global charge of a weakly gauged symmetry.  It is
a topological charge of the $\Phi_\MDL$ kink field.  The derivation (Ch.~6
\S\ref{sec:winding_qn}) shows that the topological baryon current is

\begin{equation}
J^\mu_B = \frac{7}{6\pi} \sum_{j \in \{x,y,z\}} \chi_q(w_j)\, \varepsilon^{\mu\nu} \partial_\nu \Phi_j,
\end{equation}

where $\chi_q(w_j)$ is the baryon character of tape $j$.  The current is conserved
topologically: $\partial_\mu J^\mu_B = \varepsilon^{\mu\nu}\partial_\mu\partial_\nu \Phi = 0$
by the antisymmetry of $\varepsilon^{\mu\nu}$ and the Schwarz theorem for smooth fields.
This holds without using the $\Phi_\MDL$ equation of motion.

The algebraic certificate is Lean-certified as
\lean{gte\_baryon\_number\_topological\_charge} (\CatAD, zero sorry for the algebraic
vertex scan; continuum $J^\mu_B$ conservation is \CatAD\ by the Schwarz argument).

The physical consequence is immediate.  A topological charge cannot be changed by any
local operator that does not cut the underlying topological structure.  Dimension-4
operators (four-fermion contact interactions, Yukawa couplings, gauge--fermion
interactions) are all local.  They can redistribute winding among particles, but they
cannot change the total topological winding count.  Baryon-number change at
dimension-4 is therefore impossible.

\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=orange!5,colframe=orange!60,
  title={Result: Proton Dimension-4 Stability (\CatAL)}]
All baryon-number-violating operators of dimension~4 are structurally forbidden.
The proton lifetime against any dimension-4 decay channel is infinite.

Lean certificate: \lean{gte\_baryon\_number\_topological\_charge} (\CatAL, zero sorry,
\texttt{Universality/GUTStructure.lean}).  Baryon number is a topological charge of
the $\mathbb{Z}_7$ winding field; topological charges are exactly conserved,
making dimension-4 BNV operators structurally impossible.
\end{tcolorbox}

\subsection*{Dimension-6 Operators: A Careful Distinction}

The \GTE\ theorem applies specifically to dimension-4 operators.  It does \emph{not}
forbid dimension-6 baryon-number violation, and \GTE\ makes no claim about proton
decay via the GUT channel $p \to e^+\pi^0$.

The dimension-6 operator $\mathcal{O}_6 \sim (q q q \ell) / M_{\rm GUT}^2$ involves
a heavy GUT gauge boson exchange at mass scale $M_{\rm GUT} \sim 10^{15}$--$10^{16}$~GeV.
The operator is local at low energies but is not dimension-4: it is mediated by physics
at scales where the \GTE\ framework would require a full treatment of the high-scale
completion.  That analysis is not yet complete (\CatB).  Whether \GTE\ has a GUT
embedding that would predict the dimension-6 rate is an open question.

The observational consequence of this distinction:
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Hyper-Kamiokande and DUNE} search for $p \to e^+\pi^0$, which is a
  dimension-6 channel.  Their results do \emph{not} directly test the \GTE\ dimension-4
  stability theorem.
\item Any observation of a \emph{dimension-4} channel --- which would require a local
  four-fermion contact interaction with $\Delta B = 1$ --- \emph{would} directly refute
  the \GTE\ topological baryon charge theorem.
\end{itemize}

What constitutes a dimension-4 signature in practice?  Any process where the invariant
mass of the final state equals the proton mass and the event topology is consistent with
a pointlike contact interaction rather than a virtual heavy boson exchange.

\begin{tcolorbox}[colback=yellow!8,colframe=yellow!60!black,
  title={Why This Is Not the Same as GUT Proton Decay}]
GUT proton decay: $p \to e^+\pi^0$ via a massive GUT gauge boson at $M_{\rm GUT}$.
This is a \emph{dimension-6} operator, suppressed as $1/M_{\rm GUT}^2$.  The GTE
theorem does not speak to this channel.

GTE proton stability: every \emph{dimension-4} baryon-number-violating process is
topologically forbidden, regardless of energy scale.  No four-fermion contact
interaction can change baryon number.  This is a stronger statement than the
suppression of the GUT channel, but it applies only to dimension-4.

Both predictions can be tested simultaneously without contradiction.
\end{tcolorbox}

\textbf{Falsification:}  Any observation of a dimension-4 baryon-number-violating
event --- a pointlike contact interaction with $\Delta B = 1$ at any energy, any
facility, any channel --- would definitively falsify the \GTE\ topological baryon
charge theorem.
